```
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

YMAX_DEFAULT = 20.0

def _pow2(x): return 1 << (int(x)-1).bit_length()

def full_conv(a,b):
    M=len(a)+len(b)-1
    nfft=_pow2(M)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,nfft)
    B=np.fft.rfft(b,nfft)
    return np.fft.irfft(A*B,nfft)[:M]
```

`corr_r_a(r,a)`:
```
def corr_r_a(r,a):
    n=len(a)
    rev=a[::-1]
    M=len(r)+n-1
    nfft=_pow2(M)
    R=np.fft.rfft(r,nfft)
    A=np.fft.rfft(rev,nfft)
    c=np.fft.irfft(R*A,nfft)[:M]
    return c[n-1:2*n-1]
```
Need ensure length slice 2*n-1 exclusive? c length M=3n-2; start n-1, stop 2n-1 yields n values as above. Good.

```
def compute_score(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,float)
   a=np.clip(a,0,1e300)
   S=a.sum()
   if S <=1e-12: return np.inf
   c=self_conv(a)
   c=np.maximum(c,0)
   return 2.0*len(a)*float(c.max())/(S*S)
```

```
def lowpass_noise(n,rng, bandwidth=0.05):
   w=rng.standard_normal(n)
   # FFT filter keep low frequencies
   M=1<<(n-1).bit_length()
   F=np.fft.rfft(w)
   F[1:-1]*=np.exp(-(np.arange(M//2)/ (M*bandwidth))**2)
   y=np.fft.irfft(F,M)[:n]
   return y
```
Then a=0.5+0.3*y, clip positive.

```
def run_log_lbfgs(a0, deadline, xbound=15.0, betas=(10,30,80,200,500), maxfun_cap=10000):
    n=len(a0)
    best_x=None
    a_clean = np.maximum(a0,0)
    if a_clean.max()>0: a_clean = a_clean/a_clean.max()
    x = x0_from_a(a_clean, xbound)
    best_a=np.exp(x-x.mean()); best_a=best_a/best_a.max(); best_sc=compute_score(best_a)
    bounds=[(-xbound,xbound)]*n
    for beta in betas:
       if time.monotonic()>deadline-1: break
       # estimate cost
       t0=time.monotonic()
       f0,g0 = log_objective_grad(x,beta,xbound)
       cost=time.monotonic()-t0
       denom=max(cost,1e-5)
       # assume maxfun evals; budget half remaining
       maxfun = int((deadline - time.monotonic())/denom)
       maxfun = min(maxfun, maxfun_cap)
       if maxfun < 50: break
       res=minimize(log_objective_grad, x, args=(beta,xbound), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, bounds=bounds, options={'ftol':1e-12,'gtol':1e-5,'maxiter':max(100,maxfun//2),'maxfun':maxfun,'maxls':40})
       if res.success or (res.fun < f0): x=res.x
       elif res.x is not None and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
           # accept if not huge? maybe if objective finite
           x=res.x
       else: break
       # clip x
       x=np.clip(res.x if hasattr... else x, -xbound, xbound)
       y=x-x.mean(); a=np.exp(y); a=a/a.max(); sc=compute_score(a)
       if sc<best_sc: best_sc=sc; best_a=a
    return best_a,best_sc
```

Let's design a targeted coefficient reduction algorithm:
Given a normalized sum1. C = a*a. M = max C. We want reduce active coefficients while not increasing others above M. For an active k, C_k = ∑_{i valid} a_i a_{k-i}. Products with larger a_i,a_j contribute most. If we decrease a_i and increase a_l where l not contributing to active peaks (or contributes low), active C decreases. We can perform "mass transfer" from index i to j. To ensure score decreases, choose j such that its impact on all C is small/low. We can greedily evaluate small δ.

`final_scale`:
```
def make_output_list(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
    a=np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,1e300)
    mx=float(a.max())
    if mx<=0:
       return [0.5]*64
    a = a * (1000.0/mx)
    a = np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
    if a.sum() < 0.01:
       # shouldn't; scale up if possible
       scale = 0.01 / a.sum() if a.sum()>0 else 1
       a = np.minimum(1000.0, a*scale)
    out=[float(x) for x in a]
    return out
```

```
def compute_score(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
    a=np.nan_to_num(a,...)
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,None)
    mx=a.max()
    if mx<=0: return np.inf
    a=a/mx
    S=a.sum()
    if S <=0: return np.inf
    c=self_conv(a)
    m=float(c.max())
    return 2.0*len(a)*m/(S*S)
```

`resample_sequence`:
```
def resample_sequence(a,n):
    a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,1e300)
    if len(a)==n: return a.copy()
    if len(a)<2: ...
    old=np.linspace(0,1,len(a))
    new=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    b=np.interp(new,old,a)
    b=np.maximum(b,0.0)
    return b
```

`make_inits`:
```
def make_inits(n, rng, parent, best_a):
    inits=[]
    def add(a): if len==n and a.max()>0: inits.append(np.clip(a,0,None)/max)
    if parent is not None: add(resample(parent,n))
    if best_a is not None: add(resample(best_a,n))
    x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    add(x + 1e-4) # ramp up
    add(1.0 - x + 1e-4) # ramp down maybe
    add(x**0.8 + 1e-4)
    add(x**1.2 + 1e-4)
    # concave: sqrt(x)+1e-4
    add(np.sqrt(x)+1e-4)
    # smooth noise
    noise=lowpass_noise(n,rng,0.03)
    add(np.clip(0.5+0.35*noise,1e-4,None))
    # two bumps? a = 1 + 0.3 cos? nonneg
    add(np.clip(0.5+0.3*np.cos(2*np.pi*x),1e-4,None))
    return inits
```
But many inits; score each maybe O(n logn). We can limit top. For no parent, ramp important. With parent, parent resampled likely best.

`lowpass_noise` top-level:
```
def lowpass_noise(n, rng, bandwidth):
   w=rng.standard_normal(n)
   M=1 << (n-1).bit_length()
   F=np.fft.rfft(w,M)
   freqs=np.fft.rfftfreq(M)
   F[1:-1]*=np.exp(-(freqs[1:-1]/bandwidth)**2)
   y=np.fft.irfft(F,M)[:n]
   return y
```
M from `(n-1).bit_length()`? If n=1, bit_length? use `1 << (n-1).bit_length()`; for n=1, (0).bit_length()=0 =>1. Good.

`run_log_lbfgs`:
Need return best_x. Let's write carefully.
```
def run_log_lbfgs(a0, deadline, tag='', betas=(8,20,60,150,400,1000), maxfun_cap=15000, xbound=15.0):
    n=len(a0)
    a0=np.nan_to_num(a0, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0)
    a0=np.clip(a0,0.0,None)
    if a0.max()<=0: a0=np.ones(n)
    a0 = a0 / a0.max()
    x = x0_from_a(a0, xbound)
    best_a = a0.copy()
    best_x = x.copy()
    best_sc = compute_score(a0)
    bounds=[(-xbound,xbound)]*n
    for beta in betas:
       if time.monotonic() > deadline - 2: break
       t0=time.monotonic()
       f0,g0=log_objective_grad(x,beta,xbound)
       if not np.isfinite(f0): break
       cost=time.monotonic()-t0
       rem=deadline - time.monotonic()
       # estimate maxfun, leave 20% buffer
       est=int((rem*0.6)/max(cost,1e-5))
       maxfun=max(300, min(est, maxfun_cap))
       try:
          res=minimize(log_objective_grad, x, args=(beta,xbound), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True,
                      bounds=bounds, options={'ftol':1e-12,'gtol':1e-7,'maxiter':max(50,maxfun//2),'maxfun':maxfun,'maxls':30})
       except Exception as e:
          print(f"{tag} LBFGS beta={beta} error {e}")
          break
       if np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
           x_new=np.clip(res.x, -xbound, xbound)
           # optional: if finite and not too high obj, accept
           x=x_new
       else: break
       # convert and score
       y=x - x.mean()
       a=np.exp(y)
       if a.max()>0: a=a/a.max()
       sc=compute_score(a)
       print(...)
       if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
           best_sc=sc; best_a=a.copy(); best_x=x.copy()
       # if obj not finite? break
    # final if best_x corresponds to best
    return best_a,best_sc,best_x
```
But if L-BFGS returns x with lower J but exact sc worse, and no later improvement, we still return best_a. Good.

`full_conv` for corr:
```
def full_conv(a,b):
    M=len(a)+len(b)-1
    nfft=next_pow2(M)
    return np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(a,nfft)*np.fft.rfft(b,nfft), nfft)[:M]
```
Works for float arrays.

`self_conv` use full_conv(a,a). Could optimize by A*A but okay. For real, `np.fft.rfft(a,nfft); np.fft.irfft(A*A,nfft)`; full_conv general.

`corr_r_a` uses `full_conv(r, a[::-1])[n-1:2*n-1]`. Need ensure slice length n: if n=1, r length1, rev length1, conv length1, slice 0:1? n-1=0, 2n-1=1 -> length1. Good.

`log_objective_grad`:
```
x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float).copy()
x = np.nan_to_num(x, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
x = np.clip(x, -bound, bound)
y = x - np.mean(x)
a = np.exp(y)
S = float(a.sum())
if S <= 0: return 1e100, np.zeros_like(x)
C = self_conv(a)
C = np.maximum(C, 0.0)
tiny = 1e-300
z = np.log(C + tiny)  # adding tiny may shift when C large? no effect. But when C tiny, log ~ -690.
zmax = float(z.max())
shift = beta * (z - zmax)
# shift max 0, exp_sum at least1
exp_shift = np.exp(shift)
exp_sum = float(exp_shift.sum())
if exp_sum <= 0: q = np.full_like(C,1.0/(2*n-1)) else q=exp_shift/exp_sum
logsum = zmax + np.log(exp_sum)
J = logsum / beta - 2.0 * np.log(S)
r = np.zeros_like(C)
np.divide(q, C, out=r, where=C > 1e-300)
h = corr_r_a(r, a)
grad_y = 2.0 * a * (h - a / S)
grad = grad_y - float(grad_y.mean())
# if any nonfinite, fix
if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)): grad = np.zeros_like(x)
if not np.isfinite(J): J=1e100
return float(J), grad
```
Adding tiny to C before log: If C huge 1e30, +1e-300 no effect. If C=0, log tiny. Good.
But if C has values extremely small relative to max, z-zmax ~ log(tiny/max) ~ -700-? exp shift underflows. Good.
One issue: If C=0 for some k but q maybe nonzero if all C zero? impossible; if some C zero and active max also zero? no. If q for zero is 0 because exp(β(z_tiny-zmax)) underflows unless all C ~0. If all C zero (a all zero) but S>0? impossible.

But adding tiny to C changes log for zero to -690; if max finite >1e-300, beta*( -690 - zmax) < -1380, underflows for β>=2. Good.

`h` from corr could have tiny negative? No r nonnegative, a nonnegative, convolution nonnegative. FFT noise maybe; we can clip h to 0? h theoretical nonnegative. Use `h = np.maximum(h,0.0)` to avoid negative gradient; but if noise, negative tiny. Do `h = np.ascontiguousarray(np.maximum(h,0.0))`.

`x0_from_a`:
```
def x0_from_a(a,bound):
   a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
   a=np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
   a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
   mx=a.max()
   if mx<=0: return np.zeros(len(a))
   a=a/mx
   eps=1e-8
   a=np.maximum(a, eps)
   y=np.log(a)
   y=y - y.mean()
   y=np.clip(y, -bound, bound)
   return y
```
If many zeros, eps may be large relative (1e-8), shape slightly. Fine.

`run_log_lbfgs` details:
```
def run_log_lbfgs(a0, deadline, tag='', betas=(20,60,150,300,800), xbound=18.0, maxfun_cap=30000):
   n=len(a0)
   if n<4: ...
   a0=... normalized max1
   x=x0_from_a(a0,xbound)
   best_a=a0.copy(); best_sc=compute_score(a0)
   # also score x0
   ax=x_to_a(x); scx=compute_score(ax); if scx<best_sc best...
   bounds=[(-xbound, xbound)]*n
   if n <= 8000: maxfun_cap=30000 elif n<=12000:15000 else:8000
   for idx,beta in enumerate(betas):
       if time.monotonic() > deadline - 3: break
       x = x - x.mean()
       x = np.clip(x,-xbound,xbound)
       t0=time.monotonic()
       f0,g0=log_objective_grad(x,beta,xbound)
       cost=time.monotonic()-t0
       rem=deadline - time.monotonic()
       if rem <= 5: break
       # allocate a fraction of remaining, more for last
       if idx == len(betas)-1: frac=0.9
       else: frac=0.55
       maxfun=int((rem*frac)/max(cost, 1e-5))
       if maxfun < 100: maxfun=100
       maxfun=min(maxfun, maxfun_cap)
       options={'ftol':1e-12,'gtol':1e-8,'maxiter':max(50,maxfun//4),'maxfun':maxfun,'maxls':20}
       try: res=minimize(log_objective_grad, x, args=(beta, xbbound), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, bounds=bounds, options=options)
       except Exception as exc: print; break
       elapsed=time.monotonic()-t0
       if np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
           xn=np.clip(res.x,-xbound,xbound)
           # accept if objective finite
           if np.isfinite(res.fun): x=xn
           else: break
       else: break
       ax=x_to_a(x)
       sc=compute_score(ax)
       print(...)
       if np.isfinite(sc) and sc<best_sc:
           best_sc=sc; best_a=ax.copy()
   # final score from x
   ax=x_to_a(x)
   sc=compute_score(ax)
   if sc<best_sc: best_sc=sc; best_a=ax.copy()
   return best_a,best_sc
```

```
def make_inits(n, rng, parent, reference):
    inits=[]
    if parent is not None:
       inits.append(('parent', resample_sequence(parent,n)))
    if reference is not None and len(reference)>0:
       inits.append(('ref', resample_sequence(reference,n)))
    x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    a=x + 1e-3
    inits.append(('ramp', a))
    a=1.0 - x + 1e-3
    inits.append(('ramp_rev', a))
    a=np.sqrt(x) + 1e-3
    inits.append(('sqrt', a))
    a=x**0.7 + 1e-3
    inits.append(('pow07', a))
    a=x**1.3 + 1e-3
    inits.append(('pow13', a))
    # modulated noise
    noise=lowpass_noise(n,rng,0.03)
    a=np.clip(0.5 + 0.3*noise,1e-3,None)
    inits.append(('noise', a))
    # gaussian bump? a = 0.2 + 0.8*exp(-((2x-1)^2)/0.2)
    a=0.1 + 0.9*np.exp(-((2*x-1)**2)/0.3)
    inits.append(('bump', a))
    return inits
```

```
def save_candidate(a, sc, saved, max_keep=8):
    if not np.isfinite(sc): return
    # if same length exists, update if better
    for i,(s2,b2) in enumerate(saved):
       if len(b2)==len(a):
           if sc < s2: saved[i]=(sc,a.copy())
           return
    saved.append((sc,a.copy()))
    # sort by first_key and trim
    saved.sort(key=first_key)
    if len(saved)>max_keep: del saved[max_keep:]
```

Let's test scale invariance of J under x shift with centering: In log_objective_grad, we set y=x-mean(x). This is not simply scale invariance of J over x but maps each x to centered shape. The objective as function of x is flat along constants? If add c to all x, mean adds c, y same, J same, gradient sum zero. Good.

But L-BFGS bounds on x: If it adds constant, y same but x may leave bounds. If all x shift to bound, y unchanged? But gradient along constant zero, L-BFGS should not move. Still, to avoid hitting bounds due to drift, after each stage `x = np.clip(x - x.mean(), -xbound, xbound)`. Good.

In `log_objective_grad`, we also do `x = np.clip(x, -bound, bound)` at start. If L-BFGS proposes outside? It should respect bounds, but safe. Centering after clip? But if L-BFGS at bounds, clipping may change objective non-smooth. Minimal.

Pseudo final propose code:

```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    t0=time.monotonic()
    budget=float(budget_s)
    eff=max(60.0, budget - 120.0)
    parent=None
    try:
       p=np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
       if p.size>=32:
           p=np.nan_to_num(p, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
           p=np.clip(p,0.0,1e300)
           mx=p.max()
           if mx>0: parent=p/mx
    except Exception: parent=None
    MAXN=10240
    if parent is not None and len(parent)>MAXN:
        # keep for resampling but don't output original? We'll include 10240.
    lengths=set()
    if parent is not None:
       lp=len(parent)
       if lp>=256 and lp<=MAXN: lengths.add(lp)
       if lp>MAXN: lengths.add(MAXN)
    base = len(parent) if parent is not None else 8192
    for n in [4096,5120,6144,7168,8192,9216,10240]:
       lengths.add(n)
    lengths = sorted([n for n in lengths if 256 <= n <= MAXN])
    scan_dl = t0 + min(0.08*eff, 180.0)
    best_a = np.ones(8192); best_sc = np.inf
    saved=[]
    candidates=[] # (sc,a,tag)
    for n in lengths:
       if time.monotonic() > scan_dl: break
       # If time very low for many, limit
       inits = make_inits(n, rng, parent, None if not np.isfinite(best_sc) else best_a)
       # Evaluate, maybe limit number if time per? no
       for tag,a in inits:
           if len(a)!=n: continue
           sc = compute_score(a)
           candidates.append((sc,a,tag))
           if sc < best_sc:
              best_sc=sc; best_a=a.copy(); save_candidate(a,best_sc,saved)
           if time.monotonic() > scan_dl: break
    # if no candidates (unlikely), ramp
    if not candidates:
       a=np.linspace(0,1,8192)+1e-3; best_a=a; best_sc=compute_score(a); save_candidate(...)
    # Build opt_candidates unique lengths top3
    len_map={}
    for sc,a,tag in candidates:
       n=len(a)
       if n>MAXN: continue
       lst = len_map.get(n)
       if lst is None:
           lst=[]; len_map[n]=lst
       # Insert if better
       inserted=False
       if len(lst)<3:
           lst.append((sc,a)); inserted=True
       elif sc < lst[-1][0]:
           lst[-1]=(sc,a); inserted=True
       if inserted:
           lst.sort(key=first_key)
    opt_candidates=[]
    for n,lst in len_map.items():
       for sc,a in lst:
           opt_candidates.append((sc,a,n))
    opt_candidates.sort(key=first_key)
    # unique lengths: take top but if duplicates same length beyond 2 maybe skip? len_map already top3. Choose top 4.
    top_opt=[]
    seen=0? We'll just take first 4.
```


```python
"""
Log-domain smooth-max L-BFGS optimizer for minimizing
    score(a) = 2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / (sum(a))^2
over non-negative sequences.

Main ideas
----------
1. Work in log coefficients y (with mean zero to remove scale redundancy).
2. Use a log-sum-exp smoothing of the maximum convolution coefficient:
       J(y) = LSE_beta(log C_k) - 2 logsumexp(y)
   where C = conv(exp(y), exp(y)).  For large beta this is a smooth
   approximation to log(score/(2n)).
3. The gradient has a compact FFT form and is well-scaled.
4. Multi-length, multi-initialization search, followed by local polish
   and a very final direct-convolution verification for the best few
   candidates.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

X_BOUND = 18.0
MAX_OUTPUT_LEN = 10240


def first_key(item):
    return item[0]


def next_pow2(m):
    m = int(max(1, m))
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def full_conv(a, b):
    M = len(a) + len(b) - 1
    nfft = next_pow2(M)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, nfft)
    B = np.fft.rfft(b, nfft)
    return np.fft.irfft(A * B, nfft)[:M]


def self_conv(a):
    n = len(a)
    M = 2 * n - 1
    nfft = next_pow2(M)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, nfft)
    return np.fft.irfft(A * A, nfft)[:M]


def corr_r_a(r, a):
    """h_i = sum_k r_k a_{k-i}, for i=0..n-1."""
    n = len(a)
    rev = a[::-1]
    c = full_conv(r, rev)
    return np.maximum(c[n - 1:2 * n - 1], 0.0)


def compute_score(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    mx = float(a.max()) if a.size else 0.0
    if mx <= 0.0:
        return np.inf
    a = a / mx
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S <= 1e-12:
        return np.inf
    c = self_conv(a)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    m = float(c.max())
    return float(2.0 * len(a) * m / (S * S))


def compute_score_direct(a):
    """Use numpy's direct convolution, only for final verification."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    mx = float(a.max()) if a.size else 0.0
    if mx <= 0.0:
        return np.inf
    a = a / mx
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S <= 1e-12:
        return np.inf
    c = np.convolve(a, a)
    c = np.maximum(c.real, 0.0)
    m = float(c.max())
    return float(2.0 * len(a) * m / (S * S))


def x_to_a(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    y = x - float(np.mean(x))
    a = np.exp(y)
    mx = float(a.max())
    if mx <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(mx):
        return np.ones(len(x), dtype=np.float64)
    a = a / mx
    return np.maximum(a, 0.0)


def x0_from_a(a, bound):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    mx = float(a.max()) if a.size else 0.0
    if mx <= 0.0:
        return np.zeros(a.shape, dtype=np.float64)
    a = a / mx
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-8)
    y = np.log(a)
    y = y - float(np.mean(y))
    return np.clip(y, -bound, bound)


def log_objective_grad(x, beta, bound):
    """
    Objective:
        J = LSE_beta(log C) - 2 logsumexp(exp(y))
    where y = mean-centered x and C = conv(exp(y), exp(y)).
    Returns (J, dJ/dx).
    """
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    x = np.nan_to_num(x, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    x = np.clip(x, -bound, bound)

    y = x - float(np.mean(x))
    a = np.exp(y)
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S <= 1e-300:
        return 1e100, np.zeros_like(x)

    C = self_conv(a)
    C = np.maximum(C, 0.0)

    # Stable log(C).  The additive floor only matters for exactly zero entries.
    z = np.log(C + 1e-300)
    zmax = float(z.max())
    shift = beta * (z - zmax)
    exp_shift = np.exp(shift)
    exp_sum = float(exp_shift.sum())
    if exp_sum <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(exp_sum):
        q = np.full_like(C, 1.0 / float(len(C)), dtype=np.float64)
        logsum = zmax
    else:
        q = exp_shift / exp_sum
        logsum = zmax + float(np.log(exp_sum))

    J = float(logsum / beta - 2.0 * np.log(S))

    r = np.zeros_like(C, dtype=np.float64)
    np.divide(q, C, out=r, where=(C > 1e-300))
    h = corr_r_a(r, a)

    grad_y = 2.0 * a * (h - a / S)
    grad = grad_y - float(np.mean(grad_y))

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
        grad = np.zeros_like(x)
    if not np.isfinite(J):
        J = 1e100
    return float(J), grad


def run_log_lbfgs(a0, deadline, tag="", betas=(20.0, 60.0, 150.0, 300.0, 800.0),
                  xbound=18.0, maxfun_cap=30000, x0=None):
    """
    Run log-domain L-BFGS-B with annealed LSE beta.
    Returns (best_a, best_score).
    """
    a0 = np.asarray(a0, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a0 = np.nan_to_num(a0, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a0 = np.maximum(a0, 0.0)
    n = len(a0)

    if n < 4:
        return a0.copy(), np.inf

    mx0 = float(a0.max())
    if mx0 <= 0.0:
        a0 = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        mx0 = 1.0
    a0 = a0 / mx0

    if x0 is None:
        x = x0_from_a(a0, xbound)
    else:
        x = np.asarray(x0, dtype=np.float64).copy()
        if x.size != n:
            x = x0_from_a(a0, xbound)
        else:
            x = np.nan_to_num(x, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
            x = np.clip(x, -xbound, xbound)
            x = x - float(np.mean(x))
            x = np.clip(x, -xbound, xbound)

    best_a = a0.copy()
    best_sc = compute_score(a0)

    ax = x_to_a(x)
    scx = compute_score(ax)
    if np.isfinite(scx) and scx < best_sc:
        best_sc = scx
        best_a = ax.copy()

    if n <= 8000:
        cap = int(max(5000, min(maxfun_cap, 30000)))
    elif n <= 11000:
        cap = int(max(5000, min(maxfun_cap, 18000)))
    else:
        cap = int(max(3000, min(maxfun_cap, 10000)))

    bounds = [(-xbound, xbound)] * n

    for idx, beta in enumerate(betas):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 2.0:
            break

        x = x - float(np.mean(x))
        x = np.clip(x, -xbound, xbound)

        t_stage = time.monotonic()
        f0, _ = log_objective_grad(x, beta, xbound)
        if not np.isfinite(f0):
            break
        cost = time.monotonic() - t_stage
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 5.0:
            break

        if idx == len(betas) - 1:
            frac = 0.85
        else:
            frac = 0.55

        maxfun = int((rem * frac * 0.85) / max(cost, 1e-5))
        maxfun = max(250, min(maxfun, cap))

        options = {
            "ftol": 1e-12,
            "gtol": 1e-8,
            "maxls": 20,
            "maxiter": max(60, maxfun // 4),
            "maxfun": maxfun,
            "disp": False,
        }

        try:
            res = minimize(
                log_objective_grad,
                x,
                args=(beta, xbound),
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                jac=True,
                bounds=bounds,
                options=options,
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            print(f"{tag} LBFGS beta={beta:.1f} failed: {exc}")
            break

        elapsed = time.monotonic() - t_stage
        if not (hasattr(res, "x") and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x))):
            break

        x = np.clip(res.x, -xbound, xbound)
        x = x - float(np.mean(x))
        a_new = x_to_a(x)
        sc_new = compute_score(a_new)

        print(
            f"{tag} beta={beta:6.1f} nfev={getattr(res, 'nfev', 0):5d} "
            f"dt={elapsed:7.1f}s score={sc_new:.10f} best={best_sc:.10f}"
        )

        if np.isfinite(sc_new) and sc_new < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc_new
            best_a = a_new.copy()

    a_final = x_to_a(x)
    sc_final = compute_score(a_final)
    if np.isfinite(sc_final) and sc_final < best_sc:
        best_sc = sc_final
        best_a = a_final.copy()

    print(f"{tag} end score={best_sc:.10f}")
    return best_a, best_sc


def resample_sequence(a, n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 1:
        return np.full(n, float(a[0]), dtype=np.float64)
    if len(a) == n:
        return a.copy()
    xo = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, a.size)
    xn = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    b = np.interp(xn, xo, a)
    return np.maximum(b, 0.0)


def lowpass_noise(n, rng, bandwidth):
    w = rng.standard_normal(n)
    M = next_pow2(n)
    F = np.fft.rfft(w, M)
    fqs = np.fft.rfftfreq(M)
    if F.size > 2:
        F[1:-1] *= np.exp(-((fqs[1:-1] / max(1e-6, bandwidth)) ** 2))
    y = np.fft.irfft(F, M)[:n]
    return y


def make_inits(n, rng, parent, reference):
    inits = []

    if parent is not None:
        inits.append(("parent", resample_sequence(parent, n)))

    if reference is not None and len(reference) > 0:
        inits.append(("ref", resample_sequence(reference, n)))

    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)

    a = x + 1e-3
    inits.append(("ramp", a))

    a = 1.0 - x + 1e-3
    inits.append(("ramp_rev", a))

    a = np.sqrt(x) + 1e-3
    inits.append(("sqrt", a))

    a = np.power(x, 0.7) + 1e-3
    inits.append(("pow07", a))

    a = np.power(x, 1.3) + 1e-3
    inits.append(("pow13", a))

    noise = lowpass_noise(n, rng, 0.03)
    a = np.clip(0.5 + 0.30 * noise, 1e-3, None)
    inits.append(("lowpass_noise", a))

    a = 0.10 + 0.90 * np.exp(-((2.0 * x - 1.0) ** 2) / 0.35)
    inits.append(("bump", a))

    a = 0.25 + 0.75 * x * (1.0 - x)
    inits.append(("quad_interior", a))

    return inits


def save_candidate(a, sc, saved, max_keep=8):
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return
    for i, (s2, b2) in enumerate(saved):
        if len(b2) == len(a):
            if sc < s2:
                saved[i] = (sc, np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy())
            return
    saved.append((sc, np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()))
    saved.sort(key=first_key)
    if len(saved) > max_keep:
        del saved[max_keep:]


def make_output_list(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1e300)
    mx = float(a.max()) if a.size else 0.0
    if mx <= 0.0:
        return [0.5]
    a = a * (1000.0 / mx)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    return [float(v) for v in a]


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400.0, **kwargs):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    budget = float(budget_s)
    eff = max(60.0, budget - 120.0)

    parent = None
    try:
        p = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if p.size >= 32:
            p = np.nan_to_num(p, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
            p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1e300)
            mx = float(p.max())
            if mx > 0.0:
                parent = p / mx
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    print(f"parent available: {parent is not None} "
          f"len={0 if parent is None else len(parent)}")

    lengths = set()
    if parent is not None:
        lp = len(parent)
        if lp >= 256 and lp <= MAX_OUTPUT_LEN:
            lengths.add(lp)
        elif lp > MAX_OUTPUT_LEN:
            lengths.add(MAX_OUTPUT_LEN)

    for n in (4096, 5120, 6144, 7168, 8192, 9216, 10240):
        lengths.add(n)

    lengths = sorted([n for n in lengths if 256 <= n <= MAX_OUTPUT_LEN])
    print(f"exploring lengths: {lengths}")

    scan_deadline = t0 + min(0.085 * eff, 180.0)

    best_a = np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64)
    best_sc = np.inf
    saved = []
    candidates = []

    for n in lengths:
        if time.monotonic() > scan_deadline:
            break

        ref = None if not np.isfinite(best_sc) else best_a
        inits = make_inits(n, rng, parent, ref)

        for tag, a in inits:
            if len(a) != n:
                continue
            sc = compute_score(a)
            candidates.append((sc, a, tag))
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best_a = a.copy()
                save_candidate(a, best_sc, saved)
            if time.monotonic() > scan_deadline:
                break

    if not candidates:
        a = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, 8192) + 1e-3
        best_a = a
        best_sc = compute_score(a)
        save_candidate(a, best_sc, saved)
        print(f"fallback initial score={best_sc:.10f}")

    # Keep a few strong candidates per length.
    len_map = {}
    for sc, a, tag in candidates:
        n = len(a)
        if n > MAX_OUTPUT_LEN:
            continue
        lst = len_map.get(n)
        if lst is None:
            lst = []
            len_map[n] = lst

        if len(lst) < 3:
            lst.append((sc, a))
        elif sc < lst[-1][0]:
            lst[-1] = (sc, a)
        lst.sort(key=first_key)

    opt_candidates = []
    for n, lst in len_map.items():
        for sc, a in lst:
            opt_candidates.append((sc, a, n))
    opt_candidates.sort(key=first_key)

    top_opt = []
    count_per_len = {}
    for sc, a, n in opt_candidates:
        if count_per_len.get(n, 0) >= 2:
            continue
        top_opt.append((sc, a, n))
        count_per_len[n] = count_per_len.get(n, 0) + 1
        if len(top_opt) >= 4:
            break

    print(f"selected optimization candidates: {len(top_opt)}")
    for i, (sc, a, n) in enumerate(top_opt):
        print(f"  candidate {i}: n={n} init score={sc:.10f}")

    # Allocate optimization deadlines.
    if len(top_opt) == 0:
        deadlines = []
    elif len(top_opt) == 1:
        deadlines = [t0 + 0.86 * eff]
    elif len(top_opt) == 2:
        deadlines = [t0 + 0.62 * eff, t0 + 0.86 * eff]
    elif len(top_opt) == 3:
        deadlines = [t0 + 0.52 * eff, t0 + 0.70 * eff, t0 + 0.86 * eff]
    else:
        deadlines = [t0 + 0.45 * eff, t0 + 0.62 * eff,
                     t0 + 0.75 * eff, t0 + 0.86 * eff]

    for idx, (sc0, a0, n) in enumerate(top_opt):
        if idx >= len(deadlines):
            break
        dl = deadlines[idx]
        if time.monotonic() >= dl - 5.0:
            break

        tag = f"opt{idx} n={n}"
        a_out, sc_out = run_log_lbfgs(
            a0, dl, tag=tag,
            betas=(20.0, 60.0, 150.0, 300.0, 800.0),
            xbound=X_BOUND,
        )
        if np.isfinite(sc_out) and sc_out < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc_out
            best_a = a_out.copy()
            save_candidate(a_out, best_sc, saved)

    # Perturbations in log-space around the current best.
    perturb_end = t0 + 0.98 * eff
    if time.monotonic() < perturb_end - 45.0:
        nbest = len(best_a)
        xbest = x0_from_a(best_a, X_BOUND)
        xbest = np.clip(xbest - float(np.mean(xbest)), -X_BOUND, X_BOUND)

        pert_xs = []
        for scale in (0.05, 0.15, 0.40):
            xp = xbest + rng.normal(0.0, scale, nbest)
            pert_xs.append(np.clip(xp, -X_BOUND, X_BOUND))

        try:
            _, g = log_objective_grad(xbest, 300.0, X_BOUND)
            gn = float(np.linalg.norm(g))
            if np.isfinite(gn) and gn > 1e-12:
                for mu in (0.05, 0.30, 1.00):
                    xp = xbest - (mu / gn) * g
                    xp = xp + rng.normal(0.0, 0.02, nbest)
                    pert_xs.append(np.clip(xp, -X_BOUND, X_BOUND))
        except Exception:
            pass

        # Occasionally try a different-born candidate if it is same length only.
        for sc, a in saved[:2]:
            if len(a) == nbest:
                continue
            xother = x0_from_a(resample_sequence(a, nbest), X_BOUND)
            xp = xother + rng.normal(0.0, 0.08, nbest)
            pert_xs.append(np.clip(xp, -X_BOUND, X_BOUND))

        for pi, xp in enumerate(pert_xs[:6]):
            if time.monotonic() >= perturb_end - 15.0:
                break
            sub_deadline = min(perturb_end - 8.0, time.monotonic() + 28.0)
            if sub_deadline < time.monotonic() + 12.0:
                sub_deadline = time.monotonic() + 12.0

            a_run = x_to_a(xp)
            a_out, sc_out = run_log_lbfgs(
                a_run, sub_deadline, tag=f"pert{pi}",
                betas=(150.0, 300.0, 800.0),
                xbound=X_BOUND,
                x0=xp,
            )
            if np.isfinite(sc_out) and sc_out < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc_out
                best_a = a_out.copy()
                save_candidate(a_out, best_sc, saved)

    # Final direct verification of a few best lengths.
    final_direct_end = t0 + max(120.0, budget - 60.0)
    best_direct_a = best_a.copy()
    best_direct_sc = np.inf

    if time.monotonic() < final_direct_end - 8.0:
        direct_candidates = []
        seen_lengths = []
        # Always consider the current best.
        direct_candidates.append((best_sc, best_a))
        seen_lengths.append(len(best_a))

        for sc, a in saved:
            n = len(a)
            if n in seen_lengths or n > MAX_OUTPUT_LEN + 1000:
                continue
            seen_lengths.append(n)
            direct_candidates.append((sc, a))
            if len(direct_candidates) >= 3:
                break

        for sc_internal, ac in direct_candidates:
            if time.monotonic() > final_direct_end - 4.0:
                break
            sc_direct = compute_score_direct(ac)
            print(f"direct verify: n={len(ac)} internal={sc_internal:.10f} direct={sc_direct:.10f}")
            if np.isfinite(sc_direct) and sc_direct < best_direct_sc:
                best_direct_sc = sc_direct
                best_direct_a = ac.copy()

    out = make_output_list(best_direct_a)

    # Final sanity scale (also enforces evaluator clipping bounds).
    if len(out) == 0:
        out = [0.5]

    print(f"final length={len(out)}, internal best={best_sc:.10f}, "
          f"direct best={best_direct_sc if np.isfinite(best_direct_sc) else float('inf'):.10f}")
    print(f"total time={time.monotonic() - t0:.1f}s")

    return out
```
